# Syndicate — Work Lanes

> Lanes are exclusive by file path. Two lanes may not claim the same file.
> Max concurrent OPEN lanes: 3 (see `state.md`).
> Managed by `/lane`. Do not hand-edit while a session is running.

> **History lives in `lanes_history.md`.** This file is read at the start of
> every session, so it carries each lane's CURRENT state plus one prior block --
> **plus any block that declares file claims**, which `lane-guard` reads from
> here and nowhere else. 36 superseded blocks (2,667 lines) were moved out
> verbatim on 2026-08-18. Nothing was summarised or deleted: if a lane's earlier
> reasoning matters, it is there under the same slug.

#### ORPHAN SWEEP 2026-08-18 ~21:4xZ — 8 lanes RELEASED, 32 claims dropped, contested-file invariant CLEARED

**Measured with `lane-guard.py`'s OWN `_claims()`**, not the simplified copy in
`check_lane_invariants.py` — the two disagree, and the difference decides
outcomes. The checker lacks the guard's `_is_disclaimer` / `_claimable_prefix`
handling, so it reported 70 claims / 12 OPEN lanes where the guard actually saw
**102 claims / 17 OPEN lanes**. Read the guard when the question is "is this
file guarded"; the checker answers a different, looser question.

    contested       1  -> 0           (live_gameline_join.py)
    OPEN-under-Archived  15 -> 7

**THE 7 REMAINING `OPEN`-UNDER-`## Archived lanes` ARE NOT MINE TO FIX.** Every
one belongs to a live or uncertain lane above, and the remedy is to MOVE the
block above the `## Archived lanes` marker — which is editing another lane's
block. Left for each owner. The hazard is real but latent: their claims work
today and would be dropped silently by a future archive pass.

**Method note for the next sweep.** `.syndicate/.current-lane.<uuid>` marker
filenames match archived `sessionId`s exactly (6 of 13 did), so a marker whose
id resolves to an ARCHIVED session is hard evidence the lane is orphaned. The
markers for running sessions did NOT match any roster id, so the mapping proves
death, never life — do not invert it.

- Files: syndicate/features/shared/coverage_contract.py, scripts/check_e2e_coverage.py, tests/test_coverage_contract.py
- Hypothesis: read_shortlist takes the UNION of per_sport_ingest and active_sports so an active sport cannot vanish from the matrix; that guard is one-directional, so an INACTIVE sport carrying a zero-activity ingest block gets judged and yields 4 not_reported defects each.
- Falsification test: If the fleet's mlb/nba defects come from something other than their absence from active_sports, this hypothesis is wrong and the union is not the cause.
- Verification: SYNDICATE_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:10000 scripts/check_e2e_coverage.py reports 1 defect (soccer.pregame_props) instead of 9, with mlb and nba still VISIBLE as inactive rows, and an ACTIVE sport missing its ingest block still fails.
- **`active` IS THREE-WAY ON PURPOSE.** True (listed) → judged; False (a listing exists and omits it) → shown but exempt; **None (no `active_sports` at all) → STILL JUDGED**. An absent listing does not prove a sport is out of season, and mapping that unknown onto the permissive branch is the exact failure this contract exists to refuse — so the fix does not repeat the defect it is fixing.
- Blocked by: none

### optimizer-props-tier — OPEN — opened 2026-10-14 — session 57bbae67-d845-4808-86a8-9753482aba57
- **RULE CHECK 2026-10-14 14:36Z (9:36 AM CDT), raised by the fleet-watcher session against the 2026-10-02 "every line is its own decision" rule. HONEST ANSWER: the tier is a SPORT-LEVEL lever.** It multiplies the stake (and the Layer 2 rank, since `factor_for_row` feeds both) of EVERY prop line in a sport whose own (sport, market) cell has no entry, regardless of that line's own EV. What it is NOT: a gate, pause or withhold -- admission and `value_pct` never move, the factor is floored at 0.5, and it expires in 72 h without a fresh cron. The 2026-10-05 PRIME DIRECTIVE allows measured accuracy to scale rank/stake; the existing per-cell `stake_scale` is already a (sport, market) lever, and this widens it to (sport, all props) -- coarser than anything shipped. The user chose this option in chat at ~13:05Z ("Add a sport-level props tier", option text: "would likely halve MLB/WNBA prop stakes now") BEFORE this rule was surfaced to them, so the fleet ff is HELD for an explicit re-decision. Per-line alternative if declined: fix each line's own probability (calibration of the prop model), which is what `edge_shrink` already does per cell. ALSO DISCLOSED: commit `b4108767` edited the daily-optimizer skill's review script, which lane published-negative-ev still holds (NOT among the two files the user approved moving); change = stale when today's 11:30Z run has not landed by 12:15Z. Not claimed here.
- **STATUS 2026-10-14 13:23Z (8:23 AM CDT): CODE LANDED `b4108767`, NOT ON THE FLEET.** 34 tests pass (tier accumulation, no double count, own-cell precedence, reachability through `portfolio_commit._sizing_skill_factor`). Fleet `~/Syndicate` is at `e1954b4a`; `63631afb` (lane `nba-schedule-current-season`, OPEN, owner session 2d413211 not reachable by message) sits between, so ff to `b4108767` would ship that lane's NBA fetch-schedule change before its owner measures it. OWED: `git merge --ff-only b4108767` on the fleet once `63631afb` is deployed (or with the user's go to carry it), BEFORE 2026-10-15 11:30Z so that morning's model-scorecard emits the tier. No restart needed for the producer; consumers (refresh-worker/web) load the tier lookup at their next natural restart, and an old consumer ignores the tier cell. PREDICTION (from the 10-14 report, per-cell sums, games are upper bounds): nfl tier gap -11.4% over <=236 pseudo-games and mlb -17.5% over <=96 -> likely entries; nhl <=57 games (below MIN_GAMES) and wnba props +1.4% ROI -> none. A full dry run was not possible: the ledger is in the fleet's redis and reading the worker env was refused (credential exploration).

- **MLB v1 PRE-REGISTERED 2026-10-14T13:28:29Z -- before any bought close was joined to an outcome (user: "Hits + TB + K").** PURCHASE: `scripts/mlb_props_pull.py`, the 2,379 2026 regular-season games (50 doubleheader games EXCLUDED: a team pair is not a game), batter_hits / batter_total_bases / pitcher_strikeouts at first pitch - 10 min, US region, 30 credits per game; probe 1 game (822717) = 31 credits incl. the events call, 8 books; ceiling 76,000; closes durable on the fleet via `copy_backfill_to_fleet.py --sets mlb_props_2026`. TEST (`scripts/mlb_signal_tests.py`): ONE main line per (game, market, player), most two-sided books, fair = median per-book Shin; outcome = hits / TB from the hitting log, K from the pitching log (game_type R; no row -> void; push void). FEATURES all as-of (the season walked day by day, each day's lines scored before that day is added): BvP = every pre-2026 legacy date + 2026 Statcast PAs before the day, counted with `build_mlb_bvp_index.scan_raw_chunk`'s rule; bvp_edge = BvP hits/PA - the batter's 2026 hits/PA (>= 10 BvP PA, >= 50 season PA); arsenal = sum of the box-score starter's 2026 pitch-type share x the batter's 2026 wOBA on PAs ending on that type (shrunk to his overall, k = 25 PA) - his overall wOBA (>= 300 pitcher pitches, >= 50 batter PA); recency = last-7-games hits/PA - earlier hits/PA (>= 20 earlier games); k_trend = starter K/BF last 3 starts - earlier (>= 5 earlier); opp_k = the opposing lineup's 2026 SO/PA (>= 500 PA). THRESHOLDS = discover p90 / p10 of each feature over its eligible quoted main lines (`--mode distributions`, features only, 44,547 discover lines / 1,181 games): SIGNALS (side; markets; rule): mlb.bvp_edge (over; hits, TB; >= +0.168), mlb.arsenal_edge (over; >= +0.023), mlb.arsenal_bad (under; <= -0.017), mlb.hot_streak (over; recency >= +0.113), mlb.matchup_stack -- THE USER'S EXAMPLE (over; hits, TB; bvp_edge >= +0.088 AND arsenal >= +0.013 AND recency >= +0.059, each its p75), mlb.k_trend_up (over; K; >= +0.074), mlb.opp_k_high (over; K; opp SO/PA >= 0.246), mlb.opp_k_low (under; K; <= 0.199). Also the pooled UNDER per market (H6-style). WINDOWS: discover game day < 2026-06-25 <= confirm (the schedule median). BAR: confirm fired residual 95% game-clustered CI > 0 AND discover > 0; BH q = 0.10 across the eight signals; per-market unders the H6 bar. Reported: hit-rate lift vs the eligible baseline, ROI at the best price on the signal's side. POWER, stated now: only 3,056 discover lines have >= 10 BvP PA, so the stack fires on a few dozen lines a window -- only a very large effect could pass; the arsenal / recency / K signals have hundreds to thousands.

## 2026-08-17 - THE LEDGER IS A RECORD, NOT EVIDENCE (the inverse of the same day's other lesson)

I relayed *"two uncommitted soccer fixes at risk of being lost"* to the
coordinator as an action item. **It came from a lane entry, not from a
measurement.** `git status` was empty and fix #1 was already on main.

**This is the exact inverse of the three errors recorded above it today.** There
I called healthy things BROKEN from a null lookup. Here I called a committed
thing AT RISK from a written claim I never checked. **Same root cause: treating
a statement as a reading.**

`.syndicate/**` records what was true WHEN WRITTEN. This lane was last touched
two days before I quoted it. **Before acting on or forwarding a ledger claim
about the state of the working tree - uncommitted work, missing files, a broken
service - re-measure it.** The cost here was small (a wrong action item, since
retracted). The cost of the reverse - deleting or "rescuing" files on a stale
claim - would not have been.

- RESULTS 2026-10-12 (scratch only; nothing changed in production):
  - H1 MET (hop per side, production-written sims, prod data root, 12 games 10-09..10-12 / 24 sides): every pregame side takes the history path (fallback_reason=missing_game_id: gid="" when pregame_safe, orchestrator/smart_sim.py:1436) -> bpss:_rotation_sim_minutes_for_team_local:2534 -> _rotation_sim_minutes_from_history_local:2262. The pool dies BEFORE any name map: 10-09/10-10 (written 10-09 00:35Z-12:36Z, before P2's native history backfill) team_not_in_history 5/8 + no_recent_history (preseason guard :2326-2344) 3/8; 10-11/10-12 no_recent_history 16/16. #473's `no_espn_id_map`/`no_team_rows` (1d5a521f) are the TEAMMATE-EFFECTS diag (`lineup_effects`, orchestrator/connected_game.py:234/:279), not the pool. Agrees with nba-lineup-pools FINDINGS (5) (this lane = app session ee14e4ec = CLI uuid 7693274b; both ids are the same session).
  - SECOND, REAL GAP (not this goal -- a mechanism, so a lead, not a fix): teammate effects get NO name map pregame on 24/24 sides incl. after 45e03ae2, because connected_game.py:54 is its own port of the map ending `out or _from_pbp_history()` (no native fallback; and NBA's pbp_espn_history exists only as .parquet, which it never reads). Switching it on is a mechanism added to a calibrated engine (standard: re-fit what absorbed it); DAL_HOU's `no_team_rows` also says the lineup_* tables lack teams. Needs its own lane + user decision.
  - H2 MET (REGULAR SEASON WORKS NOW): scripts/replay_nba_pregame_lineup_pools.py = production export_props_predictions_local, roster_mode pregame (production's own: smart_sim context roster_mode=pregame, asof_date=D-1, verified on 10-11 sims), ESPN boxscore STRIPPED at both summary readers, rotation_stints_history truncated < D from the live copy (P3's prepare_scratch deletes it: REMOVE_ALWAYS). 2025-26 regular, 9 dates / 84 games / 168 sides (10-22,10-23,10-24,11-12,12-05,01-14,02-11,03-11,04-08): 119/168 sides APPLIED, 119/119 reached the engine (home/away_lineups non-empty), 68/84 games with a pool. From the 3rd regular slate: 118/140 sides, 67/70 games. Name map 34-43 keys from native player_checks alone (box empty, no pbp_espn_history.csv). Every refusal is a named designed guard: no_recent_history 26 (10-22 0/24, 10-23 2/4 -- season start), rotation_mapping_too_sparse 14 (pool <5 / mapped minutes <0.5), rotation_minutes_conflict_with_current_signals 9. So the preseason-empty state is BY DESIGN and resolves itself: pools ramp in from the 3rd regular slate (10-24 15/24).
  - D-1 WINDOW (for nba-lineup-pools' function, sent to that session): pregame passes asof D-1 (smart_sim.py:1207-1213, :1507) and the window keeps date < cutoff (bpss:2321), so D-1's games are never in a pregame pool. Counterfactual with the slate date: applied flips on 1/168 sides (PHX 04-08), pool content on 48. Small.
  - LATENT CRASH FOUND: bpss:2608 (bad_rotation_total_minutes branch of the per-game-stints path) calls `rotation_sim_minutes_from_history(` -- undefined; NameError reproduced by tests/test_nba_pregame_lineup_pools.py. Non-pregame only. Fix needs a loan (file claimed by nba-lineup-pools; requested 2026-10-12).
